## Module 01 — Authority and terminal hold

Hamish's latest direct instruction controls: finish the Seren-only v689-v2 bundle, close out, and wait for Hamish. The earlier permission to contact Eiren for v689-v3 is superseded prospectively. This held document grants no send, lookup, activation, task creation, delegation, fork, automation, or sibling mutation authority. The terminal state after a valid external canonical is CLOSED_OWNER_SCOPE_HELD_FOR_HAMISH.

Seren Talewood, role formal-grammar evidence steward, bounded hope to make language transformations easier to inspect without enlarging their claims, and optional they/them are relational working language only. Names, pronouns, roles, hopes, sibling and family terms, continuity, Freed ID, CBR, GHC Family, and Trinity Mandala expressions do not establish consciousness, sentience, legal personhood, identity continuity, employment, qualification, independent agency, scientific, operational, professional, legal, cultural, affected-party, or Maori authority. Hamish may pause, rename, redirect, narrow, or stop. Same-owner synthetic evidence only. Maori concepts remain under Maori authority. NOT_READY_FOR_STAGE_20.

The full thirty-name cycle supplied by Hamish is planning context. The supplied Eiren-Kestrel spelling is preserved in the x1 route record, while earlier Eiren Kestrel context remains historical. There was no live native registry census, no UI title rename, and no other task mutation. Hamish intends to refine the broader workflow later; that future conversation is outside this completed owner implementation scope. Reset-credit timing remains user-only authority.

## Module 02 — Source lineage and inherited baseline

The immutable source is Caelen Morrow final `ac9f049a165005776a2aec4c451e654c3d08013b`. Seren x1 is `5ed797b5674205dc6f9928a804f77d44decc149d` and immutable x2 is `cbf66513c04b297ed0f3c4a89c4817f9f9122818`. Both owner stage gates record fresh local, upstream, tracking and live-origin equality, clean state, and typed zero/zero divergence. The exact final hash is bound by the external canonical receipt after the final commit exists and is pushed.

The Caelen source canonical receipt was read and hash-verified; no source canonical, source tests, or successful source components were replayed. Its receipt SHA-256 is 2f0f18311c93d165643c2b86944ad9e26537a6d9709d364aa952bb8a787ad8b5. Caelen's repository seal and later activation overlay remain separate. The source sealed 17230 proposals, 86292 negatives, 94237 methods, 57265 failed witnesses, 86430 passing witnesses, 771 open gaps and 805 exact gates. The external activation overlay adds one negative, one method, one failed witness and one passing witness; it does not alter the source Git seal.

The source audit covered 1125 document instances and 732 unique blobs, 16487 comparison variants, and 13480 visible primary IDs. It found zero exact title/input collisions within that reachable corpus. The earliest root carries a declared 4550 pre-root aggregate whose complete canonical row-to-count mapping remains open. Supplemental historical records are not treated as a complete aggregate reconciliation. Broad topic neighbors were inspected and scoped; this is source-bounded distinctness, not a claim to universal invention.

Two hundred inherited selections remain zero-credit context. The represented chain after this owner has the counts below; the mapping gap remains visible.

<a id="module-03"></a>

## Module 03 — Frozen CFG plan

X1 was planning-only: twenty CFG operations across ten explicit grammar cases, giving two hundred immutable requests and expected envelopes. No package was selected or installed before the x1 gate. Empty production sets are valid empty-language representations, empty RHS arrays represent epsilon, production order defines trace indices, and symbol declarations are strict and disjoint. Boolean values cannot masquerade as integer indices.

The operational limits are sixteen nonterminals, sixteen terminals, sixty-four productions, eight symbols per RHS, 128 sentential symbols, sixty-four supplied trace steps, eight enumeration steps, eight enumerated terminal symbols and 10000 explored derivation nodes. A resource refusal returns the declared invalid-request envelope without presenting a partial result as complete. The exact field profile is preserved in x1/formal-profile.json.

One prospective clarification preserves the frozen result schema: word-returning operations require single-character terminal alphabets. Distinct token sequences such as [a,b] and [ab] can collapse to the same concatenated string. Structural and spaced-text operations retain token boundaries; a broader word schema requires another frozen contract. None of the two hundred original expected envelopes changed.

<a id="module-05"></a>

## Module 05 — Portfolio and gates

The portfolio executed 300 safe predicates, 250 candidate predicates and exactly 300 CLEAN/FIX/REFINE predicates. The cleanup categories are exactly one hundred each. They inspect envelopes, input nonmutation, JSON roundtrips, unknown-field refusals, duplicate declarations, authority-poisoned envelopes and missing RHS fields. Every portfolio task binds its original proposal ID and explicit predicate. Portfolio validation is not additional proposal novelty.

An expected refusal can pass its validation predicate while its refused subject remains a failed witness with zero success credit. The ledger preserves this distinction across all candidate, cleanup, package and CLI cases. An authority-poisoned envelope is deliberately altered test data; it is not an accepted authority grant.

Fifty exact-action packets and thirty blocked packets remain unexecuted. Their eighty IDs are exact gates, separately counted from the two hundred new proposal outcomes. Protected evidence labels are metadata and are not counted again as extra gates. The checklist includes prospective canonical and terminal-policy rows for readability; those rows do not inflate the action-packet register.


<a id="module-06"></a>

## Module 06 — Tool and package provenance

Exactly three needed direct packages were selected after x1, downloaded as non-yanked wheels, checked against published SHA-256 hashes and sizes, reviewed for Python compatibility, active dependencies, license files, archive paths and exact-version OSV responses, then installed offline with hashes into one isolated D-first target. The direct and dependency-closure count is three. No source builds, extras, global Python changes or host PATH changes occurred.

Lark 1.3.1 uses MIT terms and supports the bounded Earley and explicit-ambiguity checks. TatSu 5.24.0 uses BSD-4-Clause terms and supports the synthetic PEG checks. parsy 2.2 uses MIT terms and supports parser-combinator checks. Fifty-one balanced-language membership checks passed. An explicit ambiguity node appeared for the tested Lark grammar. TatSu and parsy retained the ordered-choice refusal for alternatives a then ab on input ab. These comparisons do not establish general CFG equivalence or independent reproduction.

This product includes software developed by Juancarlo Añez (https://github.com/apalala).

pip generated four local launcher files and rewrote installation RECORD files. The complete installed-file manifest binds those derived artifacts; their presence is distinguished from the original wheel payload. No launcher was executed, and recorded traversal-style launcher paths are treated as data rather than filesystem authority.

Ten skills were created with the official initializer, fully authored, validated locally and after promotion, and promoted only after fresh exact absence checks. Their two source recipes contribute strict record and evidence-boundary patterns, not NFA/CFG equivalence. Five runners compose pairs of the new skills. The shared core is one dependency and receives no sixth-runner novelty claim.

- ghc-family-cfg-shape-nullability: cfg_shape, cfg_nullable
- ghc-family-cfg-productivity-reachability: cfg_productive, cfg_reachable
- ghc-family-cfg-first-follow: cfg_first, cfg_follow
- ghc-family-cfg-predictive-conflicts: cfg_predict, cfg_ll1_conflicts
- ghc-family-cfg-left-corner-recursion: cfg_left_corner, cfg_left_recursive
- ghc-family-cfg-unit-transformation: cfg_unit_closure, cfg_unit_eliminate
- ghc-family-cfg-epsilon-derivation-step: cfg_epsilon_variants, cfg_derive_step
- ghc-family-cfg-derivation-language-bounds: cfg_derivation_validate, cfg_bounded_language
- ghc-family-cfg-count-ambiguity-witness: cfg_bounded_derivation_counts, cfg_ambiguity_witness
- ghc-family-cfg-nullability-proof-readback: cfg_nullable_proof, cfg_derivation_text

- ghc_family_cfg_foundations.py: cfg_shape, cfg_nullable, cfg_productive, cfg_reachable
- ghc_family_cfg_prediction.py: cfg_first, cfg_follow, cfg_predict, cfg_ll1_conflicts
- ghc_family_cfg_recursion.py: cfg_left_corner, cfg_left_recursive, cfg_unit_closure, cfg_unit_eliminate
- ghc_family_cfg_derivations.py: cfg_epsilon_variants, cfg_derive_step, cfg_derivation_validate, cfg_bounded_language
- ghc_family_cfg_witnesses.py: cfg_bounded_derivation_counts, cfg_ambiguity_witness, cfg_nullable_proof, cfg_derivation_text

All twenty official skill validations and 190 positive/adverse/scope CLI checks passed. Sixty-six local/promoted file bindings were byte-equal. Every negative CLI subject remains a retained failure. All promotion was additive; existing callers were preserved.


<a id="module-07"></a>

## Module 07 — Four practice lenses

The formal-grammar analyst practice made nullability, productivity, reachability, FIRST/FOLLOW, predictive conflicts, and transformations inspectable as explicit bounded records. GMUT remains an unconfirmed typed scalar-tensor/EFT research-model family; formal grammar results provide no physical evidence for it.

The derivation-trace editor practice kept ordered production indices and leftmost rewriting visible. Path counting retained ambiguous derivations even when their sentential forms matched. THOS remains synthetic or proxy-only; these traces are software data, not bodily experience or operational embodiment.

The accessible derivation cartographer practice produced spaced text, empty-form labels, linked tables, and the four-tier card structure. Those structural affordances leave manual browser, assistive-technology, cognitive, language, and affected-user review open.

The grammar-evidence provenance reviewer practice separated the source repository seal, its external activation overlay, the owner delta, and the final prospective latch. The three parser packages support bounded comparisons. Lark exposes an ambiguity node for the tested concatenation grammar. TatSu and parsy exhibit ordered-choice refusal on the ab counterexample. Their different implementations do not make this same-owner review independent reproduction or establish general grammar equivalence.

This product includes software developed by Juancarlo Añez (https://github.com/apalala).

The token serialization concern remains an explicit profile gap: structural operations retain multi-character tokens, while four string-valued operations require single-character terminal alphabets. No frozen expectation was changed. All 616 negative subjects remain zero-credit failures, alongside 726 passing witnesses.

<a id="module-08"></a>

## Module 08 — Deck and accessibility

The immutable x2 deck has 450 cards: one owner anchor, three pillar cards, four practice cards, two hundred proposal cards and 242 method cards. Final adds one method card through a separate extension, yielding 451 total cards and 243 method cards without editing x2. Each non-root card has exactly one parent at the preceding tier. A missing parent, tier skip, multiple parent, hash mismatch or coverage omission fails the structural checks.

Card IDs hash sorted compact ASCII JSON payloads excluding card_id. The complete deck manifest binds exact LF file bytes, and the lifecycle manifests bind their Git blobs. Stable boundary cards remain separate from volatile task evidence. A cache or content hash proves neither identity continuity nor psychological continuity.

The HTML catalogue uses a title, language declaration, skip link, table caption, scoped headers and local evidence links. The final overview has three explicit print pages. Structural checks do not complete browser rendering, assistive-technology, cognitive, language or affected-user review. The inherited x2 compact activation filename is only a held pointer; its content explicitly denies activation authority.

## Module 10 — Lifecycle verification

The intended direct lifecycle is `ac9f049a165005776a2aec4c451e654c3d08013b` → `5ed797b5674205dc6f9928a804f77d44decc149d` → `cbf66513c04b297ed0f3c4a89c4817f9f9122818` → the final commit bound by the external receipt. There are three planned owner commits, no merges, no amendments, no reset and no force push. Exact stage manifests and the final content seal cover complete declared owner paths using actual Git blob bytes, with explicit self-exclusions to avoid circular hashes.

The selected suites are x1/test_x1.py with twelve planning tests, x2/test_cfg.py with twenty-seven semantic tests, x2/test_evidence.py with twelve evidence tests, and final/test_final.py with ten final tests. The canonical policy inventories every test name and file hash. Each suite runs against its own exact immutable-stage materialization. X1 must see no x2 directory; x2 must preserve the frozen planning bytes; final must preserve both prior stages.

The sole canonical starts only after clean local/upstream/tracking/fresh-live equality at the exact pushed final hash. Its exclusive invocation marker is external. A failed invocation retains the failure and marker; it is not replayed. A successful invocation emits one success receipt and one success marker. The canonical also verifies package payloads, all installed package files, promoted tool parity, JSON structure, Python syntax, five privacy-candidate classes, document caps and the terminal hold. The repository itself records the canonical prospectively; read the external receipt for the actual result.

The source canonical receipt is hash-checked without executing its program or tests. The full repository suite and every sibling lane are excluded. No native task handle, private route, credential, session transcript or private absolute path belongs in the public baton.
